    <script src="app.js"></script>
    <script>
        const results = [];
        function assert(desc, condition) {
            results.push({ desc, pass: !!condition });
        }

        window.addEventListener('load', () => {
            try {
                // 1. サンプルデータ投入と自動生成（2026-04-01: 祝日なし期間）
                const startInput = document.getElementById('termStartDate');
                startInput.value = '2026-04-01';
                document.getElementById('loadSampleBtn').click();
                document.getElementById('solveBtn').click();

                setTimeout(() => {
                    try {
                        const wBadge = document.getElementById('statWeekdayBadge').textContent;
                        const wRange = document.getElementById('statWeekdayWorkRange').textContent;
                        const wAvg = document.getElementById('statWeekdayAvgWork').textContent;

                        const hBadge = document.getElementById('statHolidayBadge').textContent;
                        const hRange = document.getElementById('statHolidayWorkRange').textContent;
                        const hAvg = document.getElementById('statHolidayAvgWork').textContent;
                        const sunHol = document.getElementById('statSunHolSubInfo').textContent;

                        const paidDays = document.getElementById('statTotalPaidDays').textContent;
                        const paidStaff = document.getElementById('statPaidStaffCount').textContent;

                        const workRange = document.getElementById('statWorkRange').textContent;
                        const avgWork = document.getElementById('statAvgWork').textContent;

                        assert("4/1期間: 平日日数が20日間 (月〜金の計20日)", wBadge.includes('20'));
                        assert("4/1期間: 平日出勤人数範囲が表示されている", wRange.includes('名') && !wRange.includes('-'));
                        assert("4/1期間: 平日平均が表示されている", wAvg.includes('名') && !wAvg.includes('-'));

                        assert("4/1期間: 祝日日数が0日間 (祝日なし月)", hBadge.includes('0'));
                        assert("4/1期間: 祝日範囲が「対象祝日なし」", hRange.includes('対象祝日なし'));
                        assert("4/1期間: 日・祝合計情報が表示されている", sunHol.includes('日・祝合計'));

                        assert("4/1期間: 有休合計数が表示されている", paidDays.includes('日') && !paidDays.includes('-'));

                        assert("4/1期間: 全日範囲が表示されている", workRange.includes('名') && !workRange.includes('-'));
                        assert("4/1期間: 全日平均が表示されている", avgWork.includes('名') && !avgWork.includes('-'));

                        // 2. 祝日あり期間（2026-04-29: ゴールデンウィーク）
                        startInput.value = '2026-04-29';
                        startInput.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));
                        document.getElementById('loadSampleBtn').click();
                        document.getElementById('solveBtn').click();

                        setTimeout(() => {
                            try {
                                const hBadge2 = document.getElementById('statHolidayBadge').textContent;
                                const hRange2 = document.getElementById('statHolidayWorkRange').textContent;
                                const hAvg2 = document.getElementById('statHolidayAvgWork').textContent;

                                assert("4/29期間: 祝日日数が5日間ある (4/29昭和の日, 5/3〜5/6憲法記念日〜振休)", parseInt(hBadge2) >= 4);
                                assert("4/29期間: 祝日出勤人数範囲が表示されている", hRange2.includes('名') && !hRange2.includes('対象祝日なし'));
                                assert("4/29期間: 祝日平均が表示されている", hAvg2.includes('名') && !hAvg2.includes('-'));

                                document.getElementById('test-results').textContent = JSON.stringify(results, null, 2);
                            } catch (e2) {
                                results.push({ desc: "Error in 4/29 check: " + e2.message, pass: false });
                                document.getElementById('test-results').textContent = JSON.stringify(results, null, 2);
                            }
                        }, 500);

                    } catch (e1) {
                        results.push({ desc: "Error in 4/1 check: " + e1.message, pass: false });
                        document.getElementById('test-results').textContent = JSON.stringify(results, null, 2);
                    }
                }, 500);

            } catch (err) {
                results.push({ desc: "Execution error: " + err.message, pass: false });
                document.getElementById('test-results').textContent = JSON.stringify(results, null, 2);
            }
        });
    </script>
